Update flip sign at k using current a and R:
`delta = -2*a[k]` (old a ±1)
`adds = 2*delta*a` (vector length n)
`adds[k] = 0`
`R[offsets[k]] += adds`
`a[k] = -a[k]`

Optimization loop for one start:
```
def run_sg(a, n, L, deadline, iters, alphas):
    best = eval(a)
    for alpha in alphas:
       step = ...
       for it in range(iters):
          if time>deadline break
          D = L - convolve(a,a)
          C = D.max()/(2*n)
          if C<best...
          A = D/(2*n)
          m = A.max()
          e = exp(alpha*(A-m))
          w = e/e.sum()
          grad = -convolve(w,a,'valid')/n
          # line search/backtrack
          for trial in range(20):
             y = a - step*grad
             an = project_a(y)
             cn = eval(an) # maybe full convolve
             if cn <= C - 1e-12: accept
             step *=0.5
          if no accept: step*=0.5 small break?
       # maybe anneal alpha
```

If we define weight array W_s for s=-(n-1)..n-1, with zeros outside. Then grad[i] = ∑_s W_s [ (1-h_{i+s}) for s where i+s valid (p=i) - h_{i-s} for s where i-s valid (q=i) ]. More formally:

Let's design binary local search with swap and O(n) update:
Maintain h binary (0/1) sum n/2, c full counts, C.
Precompute for each pos? To update flip at pos, we need arrays distances and neighbor values. We can use slices:
```
def apply_flip(h, c, pos):
    old = h[pos]
    delta = 1.0 - 2.0*old  # if 0->1 +1, 1->0 -1
    if pos > 0:
        d = np.arange(pos,0,-1)? j=pos-1...0
        left_vals = h[:pos]
        c[n-1 - np.arange(1,pos+1)] += delta*(1-left_vals)
        c[n-1 + np.arange(1,pos+1)] -= delta*left_vals
    if pos < n-1:
        right_vals = h[pos+1:]
        d = np.arange(1,n-pos)
        c[n-1 + d] += delta*(1-right_vals)
        c[n-1 - d] -= delta*right_vals
    h[pos] = 1-old
```
Need ensure c index n-1-d ranges 0..n-2, c n-1+d up to 2n-2. For d=pos, n-1-pos valid; for right d up to n-pos-1, n-1+d up to 2n-2. Good.
When flipping pos, self lag n-1 unchanged for binary. Sequential swap i,j: apply flip i then j. The second flip uses updated h[i]; cross between i,j contributions should be correct? Let's test: Flip i (left maybe) then j. Pair i,j: If i left. First flip i uses old h_j; updates c_plus += δ_i(1-h_j_old), c_minus -= δ_i h_j_old. Second flip j (right) uses updated h_i (new). It updates c_minus += δ_j(1-h_i_new), c_plus -= δ_j h_i_new. We want total pair change for swap i:1->0 δ_i=-1, j:0->1 δ_j=+1. old pair: c_plus term 1*(1-0)=1, c_minus 0*1=0. new pair: c_plus 0*0=0, c_minus 1*1=1. Desired Δc_plus=-1, Δc_minus=+1. Sequential: first: c_plus += -1*1=-1, c_minus -= -1*0=0. second: h_i_new=0; c_minus += +1*(1-0)=+1, c_plus -= +1*0=0. Good. If h_i_new=1? For other swap works. So sequential works.

Compute grad_h = zeros(n). For each r with weight wt>0:
  s = r - (n-1)
  if s >=0:
    e=s
    if e==0: grad += wt*(1 - 2*h)   # derivative of h_i(1-h_i)
    else:
      grad[:n-e] += wt*(1 - h[e:])
      grad[e:] -= wt*h[:-e]
  else:
    e=-s
    # lag negative: c=∑ h_i(1-h_{i-e}) for i=e..n-1. Equivalent orientation ones after zeros.
    # Term h_i(1-h_{i-e}), first i, second i-e.
    grad[e:] += wt*(1 - h[:-e])
    grad[:-e] -= wt*h[e:]

best = a_h; Cbest = eval.

def flip_update(h,c,pos,n):
    old=h[pos]
    delta=1.0-2.0*old
    if pos:
       d=np.arange(1,pos+1, dtype=np.intp)
       left=h[:pos]
       c[n-1-d] += delta*(1.0-left)
       c[n-1+d] -= delta*left
    if pos<n-1:
       d=np.arange(1,n-pos, dtype=np.intp)
       right=h[pos+1:]
       c[n-1+d] += delta*(1.0-right)
       c[n-1-d] -= delta*right
    h[pos]=1.0-old
```

So flip:
```
def apply_flip(h,c,pos,n):
    old = h[pos]
    if old > 0.5:
       delta=-1.0
    else:
       delta=1.0
    if pos > 0:
       val = h[:pos][::-1]
       stop = n - pos - 2
       if stop < 0:
           c[n-2::-1] += delta*(1.0-val)
       else:
           c[n-2:stop:-1] += delta*(1.0-val)
       c[n:n+pos] -= delta*val
    if pos < n-1:
       valR = h[pos+1:]
       lenR = n-pos-1
       c[n:n+lenR] += delta*(1.0-valR)
       if pos == 0:
           c[n-2::-1] -= delta*valR
       else:
           c[n-2:pos-1:-1] -= delta*valR
    h[pos] = 1.0-old
```
Need check right minus sign: pos left variable: c_minus -= δ*h_j. Yes.

Define `soft_max_and_grad(h,n, alpha)`:
```
c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
g = c * dx
m = g.max()
z = alpha*(g-m)
# for numerical stability, if z max large? z<=0
w = np.exp(z)
s = w.sum()
if s==0? w[argmax]=1
w /= s
grad = zeros(n)
active = w > 1e-12 maybe
for r in active:
  wt=w[r]
  s_lag = r-(n-1)
  ...
J = m + np.log(s)/alpha
return J, grad, g, c
```
For alpha high (e.g., 1e5), most w underflow to 0 except max. If tie, only one active; may be okay. But gradient from single max can lead to nonsmooth. Projection h:
```
def project_h(y, n):
    S = 0.5*n
    y = np.asarray(y,float)
    lo = np.min(y)-1.0
    hi = np.max(y)+1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       x=np.clip(y-mid,0,1)
       s=x.sum()
       if s>S: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    return np.clip(y-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
```

def weights_from_g(g, alpha, top_k=0):
  m=g.max()
  z=alpha*(g-m)
  # avoid underflow: if z.min() < -700, set? exp ok underflows to 0.
  w=np.exp(z)
  s=w.sum()
  if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
     w=np.zeros_like(g); w[np.argmax(g)] = 1.0
  else: w/=s
  return w, m

Gradient from weights:
```
def grad_from_weights(h,n,w):
  g=np.zeros(n)
  nm1=n-1
  for r, wt in enumerate(w):
    if wt <= 1e-14: continue
    s=r-nm1
    if s==0:
      g += wt*(1.0-2.0*h)
    elif s>0:
      e=s
      g[:n-e] += wt*(1.0-h[e:])
      g[e:] -= wt*h[:-e]
    else:
      e=-s
      g[e:] += wt*(1.0-h[:-e])
      g[:-e] -= wt*h[e:]
  return g
```
Loop over length 2n-1; for n=500, 999 loops; each slice op length ~250 average ->250k elements; Python loop overhead 1000. For 1000 iterations ->1M loops maybe maybe 10s. Could optimize by precomputing active r list. We'll use weights nonzero (alpha high maybe few). But for α low many. We can choose alphas not too low: [500,2000,10000,50000,200000]. At α=500, if g range 0.1, many weights maybe all; loop 1000. Still okay for a few starts? Maybe.

def smooth_optimize(h, n, deadline, max_iters, alphas, best_c=None, best_h=None):
  h = project_h(h.astype(float).copy(), n)
  c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); C=float(c.max()*dx); best...
  eta=0.2
  for alpha in alphas:
    # maybe recompute c/g/J
    for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); g=c*dx
      J, w = smooth_value_weights(g, alpha)
      grad = grad_from_weights(h,n,w)
      ng = np.linalg.norm(grad)
      if ng < 1e-12: break
      accepted=False
      # choose 4 steps
      for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
        step=eta*mult
        yh = h - step*grad
        h2 = project_h(yh,n)
        # maybe if h2 almost same continue
        c2=np.correlate(h2,1-h2,'full'); g2=c2*dx; J2,_=smooth_value(g2,alpha); C2=float(g2.max())
        if J2 <= J + 1e-13 or C2 < C - 1e-14:
           h=h2; c=c2; C=C2; accepted=True
           if C2<best_c: best...
           break
      if not accepted:
         eta *=0.6
         if eta < 1e-6: break
      else:
         eta = min(1.0, eta*1.2)
    # after alpha, maybe exact active subgradient polish: if time
  return best
```

```
def smooth_value_weights(g, alpha):
  m=float(g.max())
  z=alpha*(g-m)
  # clip z below -745? exp underflows to 0 fine
  w=np.exp(z)
  s=w.sum()
  w=w/s
  J=m + math.log(s)/alpha
  return J,w
```

Binary anneal details implementation:
```
def binary_anneal(h, n, deadline, max_moves, T0, rng, initial_best=None):
  h = h.astype(float).copy()
  S=n/2
  c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
  dx=2.0/n
  curC=float(c.max()*dx)
  bestC=curC; best=h.copy()
  moves=0; no_improve=0
  T=T0
  while time.time()<deadline and moves<max_moves:
    i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
    if h[i]==h[j] or i==j: moves+=1; continue
    # maybe ensure i is 1? Flip both signs; swap orientation independent
    apply_flip(h,c,i,n); apply_flip(h,c,j,n)
    newC=float(c.max()*dx)
    moves+=1
    if newC + 1e-12 < curC:
       curC=newC
       if newC < bestC:
          bestC=newC; best=h.copy(); no_improve=0
       else: no_improve+=1? Hmm best not obviously.
    else:
       # apply acceptance
       acc=False
       diff=newC-curC
       if diff < T*5:
          prob = math.exp(-diff/T) if diff>0 else 1
          if rng.random() < prob:
             acc=True
       if acc:
          curC=newC
          if newC < bestC: bestC...
       else:
          # revert
          apply_flip(h,c,i,n); apply_flip(h,c,j,n)
    # adapt T
    T *= 0.9995? or time-based
    if no_improve > 20000:
       # reset current to best, perturb, reheat
       h=best.copy(); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); curC=bestC
       for _ in range(3): random swap apply accept without checking? Just perturb? Ensure feasible.
       T=max(T0, T*2)
       no_improve=0
```

  if rng.random()<0.5:
     # random
     while True: i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n); if i!=j and h[i]!=h[j]: return i,j

```
def interpolate_h(h0,n0,n):
  if n==n0: return h0.copy()
  dx0=2.0/n0; dxn=2.0/n
  x0=(np.arange(n0)+0.5)*dx0
  xn=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*dxn
  return np.interp(xn,x0,h0)
```

Let's detail smooth_optimize with active/subgradient if alpha high:
```
def smooth_optimize(h,n,deadline,max_iters,alphas,global_best=None):
   h=project_h(h,n); fix; c=...; C=...; best_c=C; best_h=h.copy()
   dx=2/n
   for alpha in alphas:
      eta=0.2
      for it in range(max_iters):
        if time>deadline: break
        c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); g=c*dx
        J,w=smooth_value_weights(g,alpha)
        grad=grad_from_weights(h,n,w)
        # If grad norm small break
        acc=False
        for mult in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125]:
           y=h - eta*mult*grad
           h2=project_h(y,n); fix?
           # Optional: if np.allclose skip
           c2=np.correlate(h2,1-h2,'full'); g2=c2*dx; C2=float(g2.max()); J2,_=smooth_value_weights(g2,alpha)
           if J2 < J - 1e-15 or C2 < C - 1e-14:
               h=h2; c=c2; C=C2; acc=True
               if C2 < best_c: best_c=C2; best_h=h.copy()
               break
        if not acc:
           eta *=0.6
           if eta < 1e-8: break
        else:
           eta = min(1.0, eta*1.15)
        if best_c < 0.3800 maybe break? Could break if low.
   return best
```
This recomputes c at start and in trials; after accept, c=c2. At next iteration recompute again. Could avoid recompute if accepted and c2 known? We still recompute at top. Could structure to use current g/w? Not necessary.
Maybe too slow: For each iteration, 1 + up to6 c correlations. If early steps fail, many. Use fewer mults (1.0,0.4,0.16) and maybe maximum 2. Since projected gradient with fixed step maybe okay even if J increases? We can simply accept if C2 <= C + 1e-12? But to ensure smooth progress, maybe accept if J2<J. Let's use 3 mults. If none, reduce eta.

Now, SLSQP final:
```
def slsqp_polish(h,n,deadline,maxiter,alpha):
  global _SM_ALPHA,_SM_N
  _SM_ALPHA=alpha; _SM_N=n
  bounds=[(0,1)]*n
  cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_sm_con}]
  try:
     res=minimize(_sm_obj,h,jac=_sm_jac,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
  except: return None
  x=np.clip(res.x,0,1); x=project_h(x,n); fix; C=...; return x,C
```

Need update `grad_from_weights` loop:
```
def grad_from_weights(h,n,w):
  g=np.zeros(n); nm1=n-1
  for r,wt in enumerate(w):
    if wt<=1e-14: continue
    d=nm1-r
    if d==0: g += wt*(1-2h)
    elif d>0:
      e=d
      g[:n-e] += wt*(1-h[e:])
      g[e:] -= wt*h[:-e]
    else:
      e=-d
      g[e:] += wt*(1-h[:-e])
      g[:-e] -= wt*h[e:]
  return g
```
Good.

Binary update implement now:
```
def apply_flip(h,c,pos,n):
    old=h[pos]
    delta = -1.0 if old > 0.5 else 1.0
    if pos > 0:
        val = h[:pos][::-1]
        c[n:n+pos] += delta*(1.0-val)
        stop = n - pos - 2
        if stop < 0:
            c[n-2::-1] -= delta*val
        else:
            c[n-2:stop:-1] -= delta*val
    if pos < n-1:
        lenR = n-pos-1
        valR = h[pos+1:]
        if pos == 0:
            c[n-2::-1] += delta*(1.0-valR)
        else:
            c[n-2:pos-1:-1] += delta*(1.0-valR)
        c[n:n+lenR] -= delta*valR
    h[pos] = 1.0 - old
```
Need be careful: If both left and right updates modify overlapping slices, using in-place slice and vector on the same array. In `c[n:n+pos] += delta*(1-val)`, creates temp for RHS; in-place okay. Subsequent `c[n:n+lenR] -=...` may overlap but okay. However, if left and right plus slices overlap, when doing second addition, the first has changed c; formula expects both contributions added to original c, so sequential addition works (addition commutative). Good.
For n=2, pos0 right lenR=1, pos=0, c[n-2::-1]=c[0::-1] index0; c[n:n+1]=c[2:3] index2. Good.

Now, final `fix_sum` after potential projection:
```
def fix_sum(h,n):
 S=0.5*n
 diff=S-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)>1e-15:
   if diff>0:
     mask=h<1.0-1e-12
     if mask.any(): h[h]+=diff/np.count_nonzero(mask)
     else: h += diff/n
   else: similar
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 # one more small adjust? 
 return h
```
This may alter sum due float but okay. If after first distribute sum not exact due floating, do second iteration. Simple loop 3.

```
def make_run_binary(n,L,rng=None):
 base=np.tile(np.concatenate([np.ones(L),np.zeros(L)]), n//L +1)[:n]
 # adjust sum
 S=n//2; cur=base.sum()
 if cur>S:
   pos=np.where(base>0.5)[0]
   flip=pos[:int(cur-S)]
   base[flip]=0
 elif cur<S:
   pos=np.where(base<0.5)[0]
   flip=pos[:int(S-cur)]
   base[flip]=1
 return base
```
If rng, maybe randomize tile start. Could add `start = rng.randint(L)` rotate? Not necessary.

def interpolate_parent(h0,n0,n):
 if n==n0: return h0.copy()
 x0=(np.arange(n0)+0.5)*(2.0/n0); xn=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
 return np.interp(xn,x0,h0)
```

def smooth_optimize(h,n,end_time,max_iters,alphas):
    h = project_h(np.asarray(h,float).copy(), n)
    h = fix_sum(h,n)
    dx=2.0/n
    c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
    C = float(c.max()*dx)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=C
    for alpha in alphas:
       eta=0.08
       stall=0
       for it in range(max_iters):
           if time.time() > end_time: break
           c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
           g = c*dx
           m = float(g.max())
           J, w = smooth_value_weights(g, alpha)
           grad = grad_from_weights(h,n,w)
           accepted=False
           for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
               h2 = project_h(h - (eta*mult)*grad, n)
               c2 = np.correlate(h2,1.0-h2,'full')
               g2 = c2*dx
               C2 = float(g2.max())
               J2, _ = smooth_value_weights(g2, alpha)
               if C2 < C - 1e-15 or J2 < J - 1e-13:
                   h=h2; c=c2; C=C2; accepted=True; stall=0
                   if C2 < best_c: best_c=C2; best_h=h.copy()
                   break
           if not accepted:
               eta *= 0.6
               stall += 1
               if eta < 1e-8 or stall > 30: break
           else:
               eta = min(1.0, eta*1.15)
    h = project_h(h,n); h=fix_sum(h,n); c=...; C=...
    if C < best_c: best...
    return best_h, best_c
```

def prepare_candidate(h,n):

  h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
  if len(h)==0 or n<=0: return None, inf

      # if too long truncate/interp? simple resize by interp? Use only if len mismatch

  c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')

  C=float(np.max(c)*2.0/n)
  return h,C

Detailed functions:

```
def project_h(y, n):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float).copy()
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    if n <= 0:
        return y
    S = 0.5 * n
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        s = np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > S:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    x = np.clip(y - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return x
```
If y length mismatch n? assume length n.

`fix_sum`:
```
def fix_sum(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    S=0.5*n
    for _ in range(5):
       diff=S-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       if diff>0:
          mask=h<1.0-1e-12
          cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
          if cnt>0: h[mask]+=diff/cnt
          else: h+=diff/n
       else:
          mask=h>1e-12
          cnt=...
          if cnt>0: h[mask]+=diff/cnt
          else: h+=diff/n
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
If n=0 avoid. Good.

def prepare_candidate(h,n):
   if h is None or n <=0: return None, inf
   h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
   if len(h)==0: return None, inf
   if len(h)!=n:
      if len(h)>1:
         h=interpolate_h(h,len(h),n)
      else:
         h=np.full(n,float(h[0]))
   h=project_h(h,n); h=fix_sum(h,n)
   c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)): return None, inf
   C=float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))
   return h,C
```

`interpolate_h`:
```
def interpolate_h(h0,n0,n):
    h0=np.asarray(h0,dtype=float)
    if n0 == n: return h0.copy()
    if n0 <=0 or n <=0: return np.zeros(n)
    x0=(np.arange(n0)+0.5)*(2.0/n0)
    xn=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    return np.interp(xn,x0,h0)
```
If n0=1? x0 [1]? okay.

`continuous_to_binary`:
```
def continuous_to_binary(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    if len(h)!=n: h=interpolate_h(h,len(h),n)
    a=2.0*h-1.0
    keep=int(n//2)
    order=np.argsort(-a)
    b=np.zeros(n)
    b[order[:keep]]=1.0
    return b
```
If n odd keep floor, but we use even.

```
def apply_flip(h,c,pos,n):
    old = h[pos]
    if old > 0.5:
        delta = -1.0
    else:
        delta = 1.0
    if pos > 0:
        val = h[:pos][::-1]
        c[n:n+pos] += delta * (1.0 - val)
        stop = n - pos - 2
        if stop < 0:
            c[n-2::-1] -= delta * val
        else:
            c[n-2:stop:-1] -= delta * val
    if pos < n-1:
        lenR = n - pos - 1
        valR = h[pos+1:]
        if pos == 0:
            c[n-2::-1] += delta * (1.0 - valR)
        else:
            c[n-2:pos-1:-1] += delta * (1.0 - valR)
        c[n:n+lenR] -= delta * valR
    h[pos] = 1.0 - old
    return h,c
```
Returning not needed. For n=2,pos0 right: c[2:2+1] ok. For pos1 left: stop=2-1-2=-1, c[0::-1]. good.
Potential issue if `pos` such that `n-2` negative for n=1; not.

 return None

Binary_anneal loop pseudo with code:
```
def binary_anneal(h,n,end_time,rng,T0=1e-3):
    if n < 2: return h, compute...
    h = continuous_to_binary(h,n)
    c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    dx=2.0/n; curC=float(c.max()*dx); bestC=curC; best=h.copy()
    start_time=time.time(); nm1=n-1; moves=0; since_best=0
    min_T=1e-5
    while time.time() < end_time:
       rem = (end_time - time.time())
       if rem <=0: break
       frac = rem / (end_time - start_time) if end_time>start_time else 1.0
       T = max(min_T, T0*max(0.1, min(1.0, frac)))
       i=j=-1
       if rng.random() < 0.2:
          cmax, rmax = np.max(c), int(np.argmax(c))
          d = nm1 - rmax
          i = -1
          if d > 0:
             e=d
             if e < n:
                mask = (h[:n-e] > 0.5) & (h[e:] < 0.5)
                idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
                if idx.size:
                    i = int(idx[int(rng.randint(idx.size))])
          elif d < 0:
             e=-d
             if e < n:
                mask = (h[e:] > 0.5) & (h[:-e] < 0.5)
                idx=np.nonzero(mask)[0]
                if idx.size:
                    i = e + int(idx[int(rng.randint(idx.size))])
          if i >=0 and h[i] < 0.5: i=-1
          if i >=0:
             # find zero j
             for _ in range(20):
                j=int(rng.randint(n))
                if j != i and h[j] < 0.5:
                   break
             if j == i or h[j] >= 0.5:
                i=-1
       if i < 0:
          for _ in range(20):
             i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
             if i != j and h[i] != h[j]:
                break
          if i == j or h[i] == h[j]:
             continue
       apply_flip(h,c,i,n); apply_flip(h,c,j,n)
       cmax=float(c.max()); newC=cmax*dx
       if not np.isfinite(newC):
          apply_flip(h,c,i,n); apply_flip(h,c,j,n)
          continue
       accepted=False
       if newC < curC - 1e-14:
          accepted=True
       else:
          diff=newC-curC
          if 0.0 < diff < T:
             prob=1.0 - diff/T
             if prob > 0.0 and rng.random() < prob:
                accepted=True
          elif diff <= 0.0:
             accepted=True
       if accepted:
          curC=newC
          moves_ct +=1? (just moves)
          if newC < bestC:
             bestC=newC; best=h.copy(); since_best=0
          else: since_best += 1
       else:
          apply_flip(h,c,i,n); apply_flip(h,c,j,n)
          since_best += 1
       moves += 1
       if moves % 20000 == 0:
          c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
          curC=float(c.max()*dx)
          if curC < bestC:
             bestC=curC; best=h.copy(); since_best=0
       if since_best > 30000:
          h=best.copy()
          c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
          curC=bestC
          for _ in range(3):
             for __ in range(30):
                pi=int(rng.randint(n)); pj=int(rng.randint(n))
                if pi != pj and h[pi] != h[pj]: break
             else: continue
             apply_flip(h,c,pi,n); apply_flip(h,c,pj,n)
          c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
          curC=float(c.max()*dx)
          since_best=0
    best=project? binary no. c=correlate; C=...; return best, C
```

`smooth_value_weights`:
```
def smooth_value_weights(g, alpha):
    g=np.asarray(g,dtype=float)
    m=float(np.max(g))
    if alpha <=0:
        w=np.zeros_like(g); w[int(np.argmax(g))]=1.0; return m,w
    z=alpha*(g-m)
    # for numerical, if z has nan? set 0? g finite.
    w=np.exp(z)
    s=float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       w=np.zeros_like(g); w[int(np.argmax(g))]=1.0
    else:
       w=w/s
    J=m + math.log(s)/alpha if s>0 else m
    return J,w
```
If z min -1000, exp fine. If g has -inf? no.

def active_subgrad_polish(h,n,end_time,max_iter):

  best...

    c=...; g=c*dx; m=float(g.max())
    active=np.where(g >= m - 1e-8)[0]
    if active.size==0: active=np.array([int(np.argmax(g))])
    w=np.zeros_like(g); w[active]=1.0/active.size
    grad=grad_from_weights(h,n,w)
    acc=False
    for mult in (1,0.5,0.25,0.125):
       h2=project_h(h - eta*mult*grad,n)
       c2=...; C2=float(c2.max()*dx)
       if C2 < C - 1e-15 or np.linalg? no
          accept
    if not acc:
       eta*=0.5; if eta<1e-8 break
    else: eta=min(0.5, eta*1.1)
  final C; return best

def slsqp_polish(h,n,end_time,maxiter,alpha):
   if n<4 or n>350: return None, inf
   global _SM_ALPHA,_SM_N
   _SM_ALPHA=float(alpha); _SM_N=int(n)
   h0=project_h(h.copy(),n); h0=fix_sum(h0,n)
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':_sm_con}]
   try:
      res=minimize(_sm_obj,h0,jac=_sm_jac,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=constraints,options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-12})
      x=res.x
   except Exception:
      return None, float('inf')
   x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float); x=project_h(x,n); x=fix_sum(x,n)
   C=compute_c5(x) if finite
   return x,C

`compute_c5`:
```
def compute_c5(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n==0: return float('inf')
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)): return float('inf')
    return float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_SM_ALPHA = 1000.0
_SM_N = 250


def project_h(y, n):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float).copy()
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    if n <= 0:
        return y
    s_target = 0.5 * n
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        val = np.sum(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0))
        if val > s_target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    x = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return x


def fix_sum(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if n <= 0:
        return h
    s_target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(6):
        diff = s_target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-12
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt > 0:
                h[mask] += diff / cnt
            else:
                h += diff / float(n)
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-12
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt > 0:
                h[mask] += diff / cnt
            else:
                h += diff / float(n)
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return float("inf")
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))


def interpolate_h(h0, n0, n):
    h0 = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0)
    if n0 == n:
        return h0.copy()
    if n0 <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n)
    x0 = (np.arange(n0) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n0)
    xn = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    return np.interp(xn, x0, h0)


def prepare_candidate(h, n):
    if h is None or n <= 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size == 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    if len(h) != n:
        if len(h) > 1:
            h = interpolate_h(h, len(h), n)
        else:
            h = np.full(n, float(h[0]))
    h = project_h(h, n)
    h = fix_sum(h, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return None, float("inf")
    return h, float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))


def continuous_to_binary(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if len(h) != n:
        h = interpolate_h(h, len(h), n)
    a = 2.0 * h - 1.0
    keep = n // 2
    order = np.argsort(-a)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    b[order[:keep]] = 1.0
    return b


def make_random_binary(n, rng):
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n <= 0:
        return b
    keep = n // 2
    pos = rng.choice(n, size=keep, replace=False)
    b[pos] = 1.0
    return b


def make_run_binary(n, L, rng=None):
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n <= 0:
        return b
    L = max(1, int(L))
    if rng is not None:
        shift = int(rng.randint(L * 2))
    else:
        shift = 0
    pattern = np.tile(np.concatenate([np.ones(L), np.zeros(L)]), max(1, (n + 2 * L) // L))
    pattern = np.asarray(pattern, dtype=float)[shift:shift + n]
    if len(pattern) < n:
        pattern = np.concatenate([pattern, np.zeros(n - len(pattern))])
    pattern = pattern[:n].copy()
    target = n // 2
    cur = int(round(float(np.sum(pattern))))
    if cur > target:
        ones = np.nonzero(pattern > 0.5)[0]
        if ones.size:
            flip = rng.choice(ones, size=cur - target, replace=False) if rng is not None else ones[:cur - target]
            pattern[flip] = 0.0
    elif cur < target:
        zeros = np.nonzero(pattern <= 0.5)[0]
        if zeros.size:
            flip = rng.choice(zeros, size=target - cur, replace=False) if rng is not None else zeros[:target - cur]
            pattern[flip] = 1.0
    return pattern


def apply_flip(h, c, pos, n):
    old = h[pos]
    delta = -1.0 if old > 0.5 else 1.0

    if pos > 0:
        val = h[:pos][::-1]
        c[n:n + pos] += delta * (1.0 - val)
        stop = n - pos - 2
        if stop < 0:
            c[n - 2::-1] -= delta * val
        else:
            c[n - 2:stop:-1] -= delta * val

    if pos < n - 1:
        len_r = n - pos - 1
        val_r = h[pos + 1:]
        if pos == 0:
            c[n - 2::-1] += delta * (1.0 - val_r)
        else:
            c[n - 2:pos - 1:-1] += delta * (1.0 - val_r)
        c[n:n + len_r] -= delta * val_r

    h[pos] = 1.0 - old


def binary_anneal(h, n, end_time, rng, T0=1e-3):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0), float("inf")
    if n < 2:
        h = project_h(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), n)
        return h, compute_c5(h)

    h = continuous_to_binary(h, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    cur_c = float(np.max(c) * dx)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = cur_c

    start_time = time.time()
    nm1 = n - 1
    moves = 0
    since_best = 0
    min_T = 1e-5

    while time.time() < end_time:
        rem = end_time - time.time()
        if rem <= 0.0:
            break
        total = max(1e-9, end_time - start_time)
        frac = max(0.1, min(1.0, rem / total))
        T = max(min_T, float(T0) * frac)

        i = -1
        j = -1

        if rng.random() < 0.22:
            rmax = int(np.argmax(c))
            d = nm1 - rmax
            if d > 0:
                e = d
                if 0 < e < n:
                    mask = (h[:n - e] > 0.5) & (h[e:] < 0.5)
                    idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
                    if idx.size:
                        i = int(idx[int(rng.randint(idx.size))])
            elif d < 0:
                e = -d
                if 0 < e < n:
                    mask = (h[e:] > 0.5) & (h[:-e] < 0.5)
                    idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
                    if idx.size:
                        i = e + int(idx[int(rng.randint(idx.size))])

            if i >= 0:
                if h[i] < 0.5:
                    i = -1
            if i >= 0:
                for _ in range(25):
                    j = int(rng.randint(n))
                    if j != i and h[j] < 0.5:
                        break
                else:
                    i = -1
            if i < 0:
                j = -1

        if i < 0 or j < 0:
            for _ in range(30):
                i = int(rng.randint(n))
                j = int(rng.randint(n))
                if i != j and h[i] != h[j]:
                    break
            else:
                continue

        apply_flip(h, c, i, n)
        apply_flip(h, c, j, n)

        cmax = float(c[int(np.argmax(c))])
        new_c = cmax * dx
        if not np.isfinite(new_c):
            apply_flip(h, c, i, n)
            apply_flip(h, c, j, n)
            continue

        accepted = False
        if new_c < cur_c - 1e-14:
            accepted = True
        else:
            diff = new_c - cur_c
            if diff <= 0.0:
                accepted = True
            elif diff < T:
                prob = max(0.0, 1.0 - diff / T)
                if prob > 0.0 and rng.random() < prob:
                    accepted = True

        if accepted:
            cur_c = new_c
            if new_c < best_c:
                best_c = new_c
                best_h = h.copy()
                since_best = 0
            else:
                since_best += 1
        else:
            apply_flip(h, c, i, n)
            apply_flip(h, c, j, n)
            since_best += 1

        moves += 1
        if moves % 20000 == 0:
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
            cur_c = float(np.max(c) * dx)
            if cur_c < best_c:
                best_c = cur_c
                best_h = h.copy()
                since_best = 0

        if since_best > 35000:
            h = best_h.copy()
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
            cur_c = best_c
            for _ in range(3):
                found = False
                for __ in range(40):
                    pi = int(rng.randint(n))
                    pj = int(rng.randint(n))
                    if pi != pj and h[pi] != h[pj]:
                        found = True
                        break
                if not found:
                    break
                apply_flip(h, c, pi, n)
                apply_flip(h, c, pj, n)
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
            cur_c = float(np.max(c) * dx)
            since_best = 0

    best_h = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float)
    cc = np.correlate(best_h, 1.0 - best_h, mode="full")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(cc)):
        return best_h, float("inf")
    return best_h, float(np.max(cc) * dx)


def smooth_value_weights(g, alpha):
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(g))
    w = np.zeros_like(g)
    argmax = int(np.argmax(g))
    if alpha <= 1e-12:
        w[argmax] = 1.0
        return m, w
    z = alpha * (g - m)
    expz = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(expz))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w[argmax] = 1.0
        return m, w
    w = expz / s
    J = m + math.log(s) / alpha
    return J, w


def grad_from_weights(h, n, w):
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    nm1 = n - 1
    for r in range(2 * n - 1):
        wt = float(w[r])
        if wt <= 1e-15:
            continue
        d = nm1 - r
        if d == 0:
            g += wt * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        elif d > 0:
            e = d
            if e >= n:
                continue
            g[:n - e] += wt * (1.0 - h[e:])
            g[e:] -= wt * h[:-e]
        else:
            e = -d
            if e >= n:
                continue
            g[e:] += wt * (1.0 - h[:n - e])
            g[:n - e] -= wt * h[e:]
    return g


def smooth_optimize(h, n, end_time, max_iters, alphas):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0), float("inf")
    h = project_h(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), n)
    h = fix_sum(h, n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)

    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return h, float("inf")
    C = float(np.max(c) * dx)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = C

    for alpha in alphas:
        eta = 0.08
        stall = 0
        for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
            if time.time() > end_time:
                break

            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
                break
            g = c * dx
            J, w = smooth_value_weights(g, float(alpha))
            grad = grad_from_weights(h, n, w)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
                break

            accepted = False
            for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
                h2 = project_h(h - (eta * mult) * grad, n)
                c2 = np.correlate(h2, 1.0 - h2, mode="full")
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(c2)):
                    continue
                g2 = c2 * dx
                C2 = float(np.max(g2))
                J2, _ = smooth_value_weights(g2, float(alpha))
                if C2 < C - 1e-15 or J2 < J - 1e-13:
                    h = h2
                    c = c2
                    C = C2
                    accepted = True
                    if C2 < best_c:
                        best_c = C2
                        best_h = h.copy()
                    break

            if not accepted:
                eta *= 0.6
                stall += 1
                if eta < 1e-8 or stall > 35:
                    break
            else:
                eta = min(1.0, eta * 1.15)
                stall = 0

    h = project_h(h, n)
    h = fix_sum(h, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    if np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        C = float(np.max(c) * dx)
        if C < best_c:
            best_c = C
            best_h = h.copy()

    best_h = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float)
    cc = np.correlate(best_h, 1.0 - best_h, mode="full")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(cc)):
        return best_h, float("inf")
    return best_h, float(np.max(cc) * dx)


def active_subgrad_polish(h, n, end_time, max_iter):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0), float("inf")
    h = project_h(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), n)
    h = fix_sum(h, n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)

    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return h, float("inf")
    C = float(np.max(c) * dx)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = C
    eta = 0.05

    for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
        if time.time() > end_time:
            break

        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
            break
        g = c * dx
        m = float(np.max(g))
        active = np.where(g >= m - 1e-8)[0]
        if active.size == 0:
            active = np.array([int(np.argmax(g))])
        w = np.zeros_like(g)
        w[active] = 1.0 / float(active.size)

        grad = grad_from_weights(h, n, w)
        accepted = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            h2 = project_h(h - (eta * mult) * grad, n)
            c2 = np.correlate(h2, 1.0 - h2, mode="full")
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(c2)):
                continue
            C2 = float(np.max(c2) * dx)
            if C2 < C - 1e-15:
                h = h2
                C = C2
                accepted = True
                if C2 < best_c:
                    best_c = C2
                    best_h = h.copy()
                break

        if not accepted:
            eta *= 0.55
            if eta < 1e-9:
                break
        else:
            eta = min(0.5, eta * 1.1)

    h = project_h(h, n)
    h = fix_sum(h, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    if np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        C = float(np.max(c) * dx)
        if C < best_c:
            best_c = C
            best_h = h.copy()

    best_h = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float)
    cc = np.correlate(best_h, 1.0 - best_h, mode="full")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(cc)):
        return best_h, float("inf")
    return best_h, float(np.max(cc) * dx)


def _sm_obj(x):
    n = int(_SM_N)
    alpha = float(_SM_ALPHA)
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    g = c * (2.0 / n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        return 1e100
    J, _ = smooth_value_weights(g, alpha)
    return float(J)


def _sm_jac(x):
    n = int(_SM_N)
    alpha = float(_SM_ALPHA)
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    g = c * (2.0 / n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    _, w = smooth_value_weights(g, alpha)
    grad = grad_from_weights(x, n, w)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    return grad


def _sm_con(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(int(_SM_N)))


def slsqp_polish(h, n, max_iter, alpha):
    if n <= 0 or n > 350:
        return None, float("inf")
    global _SM_ALPHA, _SM_N
    _SM_ALPHA = float(alpha)
    _SM_N = int(n)

    x0 = project_h(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), n)
    x0 = fix_sum(x0, n)

    try:
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
        cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": _sm_con}]
        res = minimize(
            _sm_obj,
            x0,
            jac=_sm_jac,
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": int(max_iter), "ftol": 1e-12},
        )
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        return None, float("inf")

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return None, float("inf")
    x = project_h(x, n)
    x = fix_sum(x, n)
    C = compute_c5(x)
    if not np.isfinite(C):
        return None, float("inf")
    return x, C


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    try:
        parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        pn = len(parent)
    except Exception:
        parent = None
        pn = 0

    if parent is not None and pn > 0:
        if pn % 2 == 1 and pn > 1:
            parent = parent[:pn - 1]
            pn -= 1
        hp, cp = prepare_candidate(parent, pn)
        if hp is not None and cp < best_c:
            best_h = hp
            best_c = float(cp)
            best_n = int(pn)

    if best_h is None:
        n_init = 250
        for L in (4, 8, 16, 32):
            hb = make_run_binary(n_init, L, rng)
            hpre, cp = prepare_candidate(hb, n_init)
            if hpre is not None and cp < best_c:
                best_h = hpre
                best_c = float(cp)
                best_n = n_init
        hrand = make_random_binary(n_init, rng)
        hpre, cr = prepare_candidate(hrand, n_init)
        if hpre is not None and cr < best_c:
            best_h = hpre
            best_c = float(cr)
            best_n = n_init

    if best_h is None:
        n_fallback = 252
        best_h = np.full(n_fallback, 0.5, dtype=float)
        best_h = project_h(best_h, n_fallback)
        best_h = fix_sum(best_h, n_fallback)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
        best_n = n_fallback

    if time.time() < deadline - 260:
        end_q = min(deadline - 260.0, time.time() + 85.0)
        hs, cs = smooth_optimize(best_h, best_n, end_q, 70, [700.0, 3000.0])
        if cs < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = hs, float(cs), best_n

    if time.time() < deadline - 230:
        n_bin = int(best_n)
        if n_bin % 2 == 1:
            n_bin += 1
        if n_bin < 8:
            n_bin = 252

        hb = continuous_to_binary(best_h, n_bin)
        hpre, cpre = prepare_candidate(hb, n_bin)
        if hpre is not None and cpre < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = hpre, float(cpre), n_bin

        end_b = min(deadline - 230.0, time.time() + 170.0)
        hb, cb = binary_anneal(
            best_h,
            n_bin,
            end_b,
            rng,
            T0=max(5e-5, 0.0018 * max(1e-3, best_c)),
        )
        if cb < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = hb, float(cb), n_bin

        for n_try in (300, 400):
            if time.time() > deadline - 200.0:
                break
            hstart = interpolate_h(best_h, best_n, n_try)
            hstart, cs = prepare_candidate(hstart, n_try)
            if hstart is not None and cs < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = hstart, float(cs), n_try
                n_try_best = n_try
            else:
                n_try_best = best_n

            hb = continuous_to_binary(best_h if hstart is None else hstart, n_try)
            hb, cb = prepare_candidate(hb, n_try)
            if cb < best_c:
                best_h = hb
                best_c = float(cb)
                best_n = n_try

            end_try = min(deadline - 190.0, time.time() + 110.0)
            hb, cb = binary_anneal(
                hb,
                n_try,
                end_try,
                rng,
                T0=max(5e-5, 0.0016 * max(1e-3, cb)),
            )
            if cb < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = hb, float(cb), n_try

    if time.time() < deadline - 18:
        n_pol = int(best_n)
        maxit = 130 if n_pol <= 350 else 75
        end_pol = min(deadline - 18.0, time.time() + 130.0)
        hs, cs = smooth_optimize(
            best_h,
            n_pol,
            end_pol,
            maxit,
            [1000.0, 5000.0, 20000.0],
        )
        if cs < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = hs, float(cs), n_pol

        if best_n <= 350 and time.time() < deadline - 12:
            hs2, cs2 = active_subgrad_polish(best_h, int(best_n), min(deadline - 12.0, time.time() + 55.0), 70)
            if cs2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = hs2, float(cs2), int(best_n)

        if best_n <= 350 and time.time() < deadline - 12:
            h_noise = project_h(best_h + 0.045 * rng.randn(int(best_n)), int(best_n))
            hs3, cs3 = smooth_optimize(h_noise, int(best_n), min(deadline - 12.0, time.time() + 55.0), 45, [5000.0, 20000.0])
            if cs3 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = hs3, float(cs3), int(best_n)

    if best_n <= 300 and time.time() < deadline - 8:
        hs4, cs4 = slsqp_polish(best_h, int(best_n), 55, 3000.0)
        if hs4 is not None and cs4 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = hs4, float(cs4), int(best_n)

    best_h = project_h(np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float).copy(), int(best_n))
    best_h = fix_sum(best_h, int(best_n))
    final_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    if not np.isfinite(final_c):
        final_c = float("inf")
        best_h = np.full(252, 0.5, dtype=float)
        best_h = project_h(best_h, 252)
        best_h = fix_sum(best_h, 252)
        final_c = compute_c5(best_h)
        best_n = 252

    return best_h, float(final_c), int(best_n)
```
